", date phrases), calls Edge Function → Anthropic API → extracts structured filters (sender, date range, keywords, folder) → Postgres query. Displays parsed filter chips above results. Fallback to plain search on AI failure.\n\n**3.5.8 Spam UX:**\n- Non-intrusive banner on inbox messages with pending spam_flag\n- Actions: \\"Move to Spam\\" or \\"Not Spam\\"\n- Never auto-move silently\n\n### 3.6 Browser Push Notifications\n\n**Notification Permission Handling:**\n- On first login or when user navigates to /inbox/settings, display prompt to enable push notifications\n- Request browser notification permission via Notification API\n- Store user preference in notification_preferences table\n\n**Push Notification Triggers:**\n- When sync service inserts new message into messages table, trigger push notification via Supabase Realtime or Edge Function\n- Notification payload includes: sender name, subject, snippet preview\n\n**Notification Behavior:**\n- Display native desktop/browser alert with sender, subject, snippet\n- Click notification opens corresponding thread in /inbox reading pane\n- Optional sound indicator (configurable in settings)\n- Optional badge indicator on browser tab/icon (unread count)\n\n**Settings Integration:**\n- /inbox/settings includes Push Notification Settings section\n- Toggle: Enable/Disable push notifications\n- Toggle: Enable/Disable notification sound\n- Toggle: Enable/Disable badge indicator\n\n**Service Worker:**\n- Register service worker for push notification handling\n- Service worker listens for push events and displays notifications\n- Service worker handles notification click events to open thread\n\n### 3.7 Contacts Management\n\n**3.7.1 /inbox/contacts - Contact List:**\n- Display table with columns: name, email, company, phone\n- Actions: Add Contact, Edit, Delete\n- Auto-suggest adding contact on reply to new address\n\n**3.7.2 Contact CRUD Forms:**\n- Fields: name, email, company, phone, notes\n- Submit creates/updates contacts row\n\n### 3.8 Calendar\n\n**3.8.1 /inbox/calendar - Calendar Views:**\n\n**Month/Week View:**\n- Month/week view toggle\n- Display events from calendar_events table\n- Color-code events by department (HR, Finance, Operations, General)\n- Render tasks (is_task=true) as checkbox-style items (not timed blocks)\n- Allow toggling is_completed directly from calendar view\n- Completed tasks visually gray out/strike through but remain visible\n- Expand recurring events client-side using rrule.js\n- Actions: Add Event, Edit, Delete\n\n**Department Filter Toggle:**\n- Filter/toggle to show/hide events by department (HR, Finance, Operations, General)\n\n**Free/Busy View:**\n- Display all staff as horizontal rows across day/week timeline\n- Show colored Busy blocks for confirmed events\n- Show tentative events with diagonal stripes\n- No event titles/descriptions exposed in this view\n\n**Resource Availability View:**\n- Display resources as horizontal rows across day/week timeline\n- Show booked time slots with resource name\n- Indicate conflicts when resource is double-booked\n\n**3.8.2 Event CRUD Forms:**\n\n**Fields:**\n- Title\n- Agenda (renamed from description)\n- Start date/time\n- End date/time\n- Location\n- Attendees (text array)\n- Department (dropdown: HR, Finance, Operations, General — default General)\n- Status (dropdown: confirmed, tentative, cancelled)\n- Is Task (checkbox, default false)\n- Is Completed (checkbox, visible only if is_task=true, default false)\n- Recurrence Rule (dropdown: None/Daily/Weekly/Monthly/Custom, opens recurrence picker)\n- Recurrence End Date (visible only if recurrence rule set)\n- Reminder (dropdown: None/10 minutes/30 minutes/60 minutes before)\n- Book a Resource (dropdown with conflict detection)\n- Attach File (file upload control)\n\n**Recurrence Picker:**\n- None: no recurrence\n- Daily: repeat every N days\n- Weekly: repeat every N weeks on selected days\n- Monthly: repeat every N months on selected day\n- Custom: RRULE string input for advanced patterns\n\n**Conflict Detection:**\n- When booking resource, check resource_bookings for overlapping time slots\n- Display warning if conflict detected\n\n**Editing Recurring Events:**\n- Prompt user: \\"Edit this occurrence\\" or \\"Edit entire series\\"\n- If \\"this occurrence\\", create new event with parent_event_id\n- If \\"entire series\\", update parent event and all future occurrences\n\n**Attached Files:**\n- Display list of attached files in event detail view\n- Allow download/delete of attachments\n\n**Submit:**\n- Creates/updates calendar_events row\n- Creates resource_bookings row if resource selected\n- Uploads attachments to Supabase Storage, creates calendar_event_attachments rows\n- If external attendees (non-staff emails) present, generate .ics file and queue email with attachment\n\n### 3.9 Settings\n\n**3.9.1 /inbox/settings - Settings Page:**\n\n**Signatures Management:**\n\n**Signature List:**\n- Display list of signatures per mailbox\n- Actions: Add Signature, Edit, Delete\n- Show signature name, is_default flag\n\n**Add/Edit Signature Form:**\n\n**Rich Text Editor:**\n- Reuse the same Tiptap-based rich text editor component from Compose as a shared component\n- Include the same formatting toolbar:\n  + Text formatting: Bold, Italic, Underline, Strikethrough\n  + Font family dropdown: sans-serif, serif, monospace\n  + Font size dropdown: small, normal, large, huge\n  + Text color picker\n  + Highlight/background color picker\n  + Alignment: left, center, right, justify\n  + Lists: ordered list, unordered list, indent, outdent\n  + Insert link\n  + Insert emoji\n  + Insert inline image\n  + Remove formatting button\n  + Blockquote toggle\n\n**Image Insert and Resize:**\n- Image insert control in toolbar (same upload mechanism as Compose)\n- Images stored in Supabase Storage, referenced via <img> tag in signature HTML\n- After image inserted, display resize handles (corner-drag) for direct in-editor resizing\n- Enforce maximum width cap (e.g., 300px) to prevent oversized signature images\n- Save resized dimensions as explicit width and height HTML attributes on <img> tag (not just CSS)\n\n**Live Preview Panel:**\n- Display live preview below or beside signature editor\n- Preview shows signature as it will render after CSS-inlining pipeline (Prompt 09 logic) is applied\n- Preview updates in real-time as user edits signature\n- Ensures user sees final rendered output matching what recipients will see\n\n**Fields:**\n- Signature name (optional, for user reference)\n- is_default flag (checkbox)\n- body_html (rich text editor output)\n\n**Submit:**\n- Creates/updates signatures row\n- Signature auto-appended to outgoing emails as before\n\n**Linked Mailboxes (read-only):**\n- Display list of mailboxes linked to current user\n\n**Rules & Filters:**\n- List active rules\n- Add/Edit/Delete rule\n- Fields: condition_json (from/subject contains), action_json (label/move/mark-read), is_active flag\n- Rules applied in sync service after inserting new message\n\n**Out-of-Office:**\n- Toggle autoresponder on/off\n- Fields: subject, body, optional start/end dates\n- Calls Edge Function → cPanel UAPI Email::add_autoresponder\n\n**Notification Preferences:**\n- Toggle: Enable/Disable email notifications\n- Toggle: Enable/Disable desktop notifications\n\n**Push Notification Settings:**\n- Toggle: Enable/Disable push notifications\n- Toggle: Enable/Disable notification sound\n- Toggle: Enable/Disable badge indicator\n- Button: Request notification permission (if not granted)\n\n### 3.10 Sync Service\n\n**Location:** /sync-service/ directory\n\n**Structure:**\n- src/index.ts — entrypoint, starts sync loop for all active mailboxes\n- src/imap-client.ts — ImapFlow wrapper: connect, backfill, IDLE watch\n- src/smtp-client.ts — nodemailer wrapper for send + email-safe send pipeline\n- src/folder-mapper.ts — maps IMAP folder names to normalized_type\n- src/spam-detector.ts — parses X-Spam-Score/X-Spam-Status headers + AI second-pass\n- src/supabase-sync.ts — writes parsed mail into Postgres + Storage\n- src/credential-vault.ts — retrieves decrypted credentials from Supabase Vault\n- src/push-notifier.ts — triggers push notifications via Supabase Realtime or Edge Function\n- src/email-safe-pipeline.ts — CSS inlining + plain text generation before SMTP send\n- src/reminder-scheduler.ts — checks for due reminders every 1-2 minutes\n- src/ics-generator.ts — generates .ics files for calendar invites\n- Dockerfile, package.json\n\n**Sync Behaviors:**\n\n**Multi-Folder Real-Time Sync:**\n- For each active mailbox, maintain multiple simultaneous IMAP connections — one per folder\n- High-priority folders (Inbox, Sent, Drafts, Archive): dedicated IDLE connection each\n- Lower-priority folders (Trash, Spam): polling every 60-120 seconds\n- Initial backfill: last 90 days across all folders on first connect; sync_status \'syncing\' → \'active\'\n- Folder discovery via IMAP LIST → populate mailbox_folders\n- Messages from all folders stored with correct folder metadata (folder_id references mailbox_folders)\n- Deduplication via unique(mailbox_id, imap_uid, imap_uidvalidity) applies to all folders\n- SMTP-sent messages that later appear in IMAP Sent folder handled by deduplication constraint\n\n**General Sync Logic:**\n- Threading via In-Reply-To/References headers, fallback to normalized subject matching\n- Attachments extracted, uploaded to Supabase Storage at attachments/{mailbox_id}/{message_id}/{filename}\n- Error handling: set sync_status=\'error\', last_error on connection failure\n- SMTP send via sync service nodemailer with email-safe send pipeline; immediately inserts sent message to DB\n- Spam Layer 1: parse X-Spam-Score/X-Spam-Status headers; mail already in Junk folder → spam_status=\'confirmed_spam\'\n- Spam Layer 2: AI second-pass via Anthropic API for inbox messages with borderline spam signals; insert spam_flags row with source=\'ai_second_pass\', confidence, reason; NEVER auto-move\n- Rules engine: after inserting new message, check active rules for that mailbox and apply actions\n- Scheduled send: check scheduled_messages every minute for due sends\n- Push notification trigger: after inserting new message, call push-notifier to send browser push notification to user\n\n**Email-Safe Send Pipeline:**\n- Runs immediately before SMTP delivery in sync service\n- CSS Inlining: Uses juice library to convert body_html CSS (classes, style blocks, rich text editor output) into inline style attributes\n- Plain Text Fallback: Uses html-to-text library to generate clean plain-text version of composed HTML\n- Multipart/Alternative: Sends every outbound email with both text and html parts via nodemailer\n- Email-Safe Guardrails:\n  + Strip unsupported CSS properties (position, flex, grid, background-image) during inlining\n  + Ensure inline img tags include explicit width and height HTML attributes\n  + Resolve font-family declarations to web-safe fallback stacks (Arial, Helvetica, sans-serif)\n- Drafts remain in original editable form; conversion only applied to final sent copy\n- Sent copy stored in messages.body_html matches transmitted version\n\n**Reminder Scheduler:**\n- Checks calendar_events every 1-2 minutes for due reminders (reminder_minutes_before not null, reminder_sent_at null, start_at within reminder window)\n- Delivers in-app notification/toast via Supabase Realtime\n- Updates reminder_sent_at timestamp after delivery\n\n**.ics Invite Generation:**\n- When creating event with external attendees (non-staff emails), generate .ics file using ics npm library\n- Include organizer, attendees, title, agenda, location, start/end, RRULE if recurring\n- Attach .ics file to email via existing SMTP/queue pipeline\n- One-way only (no incoming .ics parsing)\n\n### 3.11 Edge Functions\n\n**store-mailbox-credentials:**\n- Saves password to Supabase Vault\n- Writes credential_vault_ref to mailboxes table\n\n**invite-staff-user:**\n- Creates Supabase auth user via admin API\n- Creates staff_users row\n- Sends invite email\n\n**send-email:**\n- Queues outbound message for sync service to process via email-safe send pipeline\n- Alternative: If sync service handles SMTP directly, this function may be deprecated\n\n**summarize-thread:**\n- Anthropic API call for thread summarization (threads with 3+ messages only)\n- Stores in ai_cache table\n- Returns concise neutral summary (key points, action items, status)\n\n**smart-search:**\n- Anthropic API call to parse natural-language query into structured filters\n\n**cpanel-autoresponder:**\n- Calls cPanel UAPI to set/clear autoresponder\n\n**track-read-receipt:**\n- Marks read_receipt_confirmed_at on message\n- Optional tracking pixel endpoint\n\n**send-push-notification:**\n- Sends browser push notification to user via Push API\n- Payload includes sender name, subject, snippet preview\n\n**bulk-update-placeholders:**\n- Accepts placeholder name, new value, and list of user IDs\n- Updates signatures.body_html for all selected users by replacing placeholder with new value\n- Returns success/failure status for each user\n\n### 3.12 Responsiveness\n\n- Fully responsive design\n- Mobile: single-pane navigation pattern (folder list → thread list → reading pane with back nav)\n- Compose window: on tablet/mobile viewports, falls back to full-width single-pane layout\n- PWA-friendly\n- Landing page fully responsive with mobile-optimized layout\n\n### 3.13 Keyboard Shortcuts\n\n- c: compose\n- r: reply\n- a: reply-all\n- f: forward\n- e: archive\n- #: delete\n- j/k: navigate thread list\n\n## 4. Business Rules and Logic\n\n### 4.1 Multi-Tenant Architecture\n\n- Database schema supports multiple organizations from day 1\n- Phase 1 has one org (Frimps Oil Company)\n- All tables with RLS (Row Level Security)\n- organizations table stores branding_config (jsonb: primary_color, accent_color, surface_color, logo_url, theme_mode)\n\n### 4.2 Database Schema\n\n**organizations:**\n- id, name, domain, branding_config (jsonb), created_at\n\n**staff_users:**\n- id (refs auth.users), organization_id, full_name, role (\'admin\'|\'staff\'), created_at\n\n**mailboxes:**\n- id, organization_id, staff_user_id (nullable), email_address, display_name, imap_host, imap_port(993), smtp_host, smtp_port(587), credential_vault_ref, sync_status(\'pending\'|\'syncing\'|\'active\'|\'error\'), last_synced_at, last_error, created_at\n\n**mailbox_folders:**\n- id, mailbox_id, imap_folder_name, normalized_type(\'inbox\'|\'sent\'|\'drafts\'|\'trash\'|\'spam\'|\'archive\'|\'custom\'), display_name\n\n**threads:**\n- id, mailbox_id, subject, participants(text[]), last_message_at, is_read, is_starred, labels(text[]), folder_id, snoozed_until(timestamptz)\n\n**messages:**\n- id, thread_id, mailbox_id, imap_uid(bigint), imap_uidvalidity(bigint), from_address, from_name, to_addresses(text[]), cc_addresses(text[]), bcc_addresses(text[]), body_html, body_text, sent_at, is_read, is_flagged, spam_score(numeric), spam_status(\'clean\'|\'flagged\'|\'confirmed_spam\'), raw_headers(jsonb), read_receipt_confirmed_at(timestamptz)\n- unique(mailbox_id, imap_uid, imap_uidvalidity)\n\n**attachments:**\n- id, message_id, storage_path, filename, mime_type, size_bytes\n\n**ai_cache:**\n- id, thread_id, type(\'summary\'|\'draft_suggestion\'), content, generated_at\n\n**spam_flags:**\n- id, message_id, source(\'spamassassin\'|\'ai_second_pass\'), confidence, reason, user_action(\'pending\'|\'confirmed\'|\'dismissed\')\n\n**signatures:**\n- id, mailbox_id, body_html, is_default\n\n**contacts:**\n- id, organization_id, name, email, company, phone, notes, created_by\n\n**rules:**\n- id, mailbox_id, condition_json, action_json, is_active\n\n**calendar_events:**\n- id, organization_id, created_by, title, agenda, start_at, end_at, location, attendees(text[]), department(text: HR|Finance|Operations|General, default General), status(text: confirmed|tentative|cancelled), is_task(boolean, default false), is_completed(boolean, default false), recurrence_rule(text, RRULE string), recurrence_end_date(date), parent_event_id(nullable, refs calendar_events.id), reminder_minutes_before(integer, nullable), reminder_sent_at(timestamptz, nullable)\n\n**calendar_event_attachments:**\n- id, event_id, storage_path, filename, mime_type, size_bytes\n\n**resources:**\n- id, organization_id, name, type(text: room|vehicle|equipment|other), description, is_active(boolean, default true)\n\n**resource_bookings:**\n- id, resource_id, calendar_event_id, start_at, end_at\n\n**scheduled_messages:**\n- id, mailbox_id, to_addresses(text[]), cc_addresses(text[]), subject, body_html, attachments_json, send_at, sent_at(nullable), status\n\n**feature_interest:**\n- id, staff_user_id, feature, created_at\n\n**notification_preferences:**\n- id, staff_user_id, push_enabled(boolean), sound_enabled(boolean), badge_enabled(boolean), created_at, updated_at\n\n### 4.3 Branding System\n\n- CSS variables: --cosmos-bg, --cosmos-primary, --cosmos-accent, --cosmos-surface, --cosmos-text\n- ThemeProvider reads from logged-in user\'s org branding_config OR pre-auth domain detection\n- Frimps Oil branding_config: {\\"primary_color\\": \\"#E31E24\\", \\"accent_color\\": \\"#F7941D\\", \\"surface_color\\": \\"#FFFFFF\\", \\"theme_mode\\": \\"light\\", \\"logo_url\\": \\"<storage path>\\"} seeded in organizations table\n- Default Cosmos Mail theme: deep indigo #1B1F3B background, violet/indigo accents, dark mode\n- All brand colors applied via CSS variables only — never hardcoded hex in components\n- Landing page uses Frimps Oil branding for Phase 1\n\n### 4.4 AI Features\n\n**Thread Summarization:**\n- \\"Summarize\\" button in reading pane (threads with 3+ messages only)\n- Edge Function → Anthropic API → concise neutral summary (key points, action items, status)\n- Cache in ai_cache; serve cached unless new messages since generated_at or user clicks \\"Regenerate\\"\n- Display in collapsible card at top of reading pane, accent border, \\"AI Summary\\" label\n\n**Smart Search:**\n- Detect natural-language queries (heuristic: contains \\"from\\", \\"last\\", \\"about\\", date phrases)\n- Edge Function → Anthropic API → extract structured filters (sender, date range, keywords, folder) → Postgres query\n- Show parsed filter chips above results; fallback to plain search on AI failure\n- Never show blank results\n\n### 4.5 Spam Detection\n\n**Layer 1:**\n- Parse X-Spam-Score/X-Spam-Status headers from mail server\n- Mail already in Junk folder → spam_status=\'confirmed_spam\'\n\n**Layer 2:**\n- AI second-pass via Anthropic API for inbox messages with borderline spam signals\n- Insert spam_flags row with source=\'ai_second_pass\', confidence, reason\n- NEVER auto-move to spam folder\n- Display non-intrusive banner with \\"Move to Spam\\" or \\"Not Spam\\" actions\n\n### 4.6 Rules Engine\n\n- After inserting new message, check active rules for that mailbox\n- Apply actions based on condition_json (from/subject contains) and action_json (label/move/mark-read)\n\n### 4.7 Scheduled Send\n\n- Check scheduled_messages table every minute for due sends\n- Send via email-safe send pipeline when send_at time passes\n- Update sent_at and status fields\n\n### 4.8 Read Receipts\n\n- Optional tracking pixel via Edge Function endpoint\n- Best-effort, clearly labeled in UI\n- Marks read_receipt_confirmed_at on message when pixel loaded\n\n### 4.9 Out-of-Office\n\n- Calls cPanel UAPI Email::add_autoresponder via Edge Function\n- Toggle on/off, set subject, body, optional start/end dates\n\n### 4.10 Push Notifications\n\n**Permission Handling:**\n- Request browser notification permission on first login or in settings\n- Store user preference in notification_preferences table\n\n**Notification Triggers:**\n- Sync service triggers push notification when new message inserted\n- Edge Function sends push notification via Push API\n\n**Notification Content:**\n- Sender name, subject, snippet preview\n- Click opens thread in /inbox reading pane\n\n**Settings:**\n- User can enable/disable push notifications, sound, badge indicator in /inbox/settings\n\n**Service Worker:**\n- Register service worker for push notification handling\n- Service worker listens for push events and displays notifications\n- Service worker handles notification click events to open thread\n\n### 4.11 Landing Page Routing\n\n**Public Route (/):**\n- Default route for unauthenticated users\n- Displays marketing landing page with integrated sign-in panel\n- On successful login, redirect to /inbox\n\n**Authenticated User Behavior:**\n- Authenticated users accessing / are redirected to /inbox\n\n**Existing /login Route:**\n- Can redirect to / or remain functional as fallback\n\n### 4.12 Compose Window Rich Text\n\n**Rich Text Output:**\n- Tiptap editor output stored as body_html in messages table\n- Inline images stored via Supabase Storage, referenced in body_html with storage URLs\n\n**Reply/Forward Compatibility:**\n- Existing quoting logic preserved when replying/forwarding\n- Quoted content renders correctly inside Tiptap editor\n- Original message formatting maintained in quoted sections\n\n**External Client Compatibility:**\n- Rich text emails render correctly in Gmail, Outlook, Apple Mail via email-safe send pipeline\n- HTML email output follows standard email HTML conventions\n- Graceful degradation for clients with limited HTML support\n\n### 4.13 Multi-Folder Sync Logic\n\n**Folder Sync Coverage:**\n- High-priority folders (Inbox, Sent, Drafts, Archive): dedicated IDLE connection each\n- Lower-priority folders (Trash, Spam): polling every 60-120s\n- All folders synced automatically for all current and future mailboxes\n\n**Folder Metadata:**\n- Each message stored with folder_id referencing mailbox_folders table\n- Sent messages appear under Sent folder in UI\n- Drafts appear under Drafts folder\n- Junk messages appear under Spam folder\n- Trash messages appear under Trash folder\n\n**Polling Strategy:**\n- Same robust polling mechanism used for INBOX applied to all folders\n- Handles UIDVALIDITY resets per folder\n- Deduplication via unique constraint per folder\n\n**SMTP-Sent Message Handling:**\n- SMTP-sent messages immediately inserted to DB with folder_id for Sent folder\n- When same message later appears in IMAP Sent folder, deduplication constraint prevents duplicate insert\n\n### 4.14 Email-Safe Send Pipeline\n\n**Pipeline Location:**\n- Runs in sync service (Node.js environment) immediately before SMTP delivery\n- If current implementation uses Supabase Edge Function with Deno, move SMTP send to sync service for library compatibility\n\n**CSS Inlining:**\n- Uses juice library to convert body_html CSS into inline style attributes\n- Conversion happens at send time only, not at compose or draft-save time\n- Drafts remain in original editable form with classes and style blocks\n\n**Plain Text Generation:**\n- Uses html-to-text library to auto-generate plain-text version from body_html\n- No manual plain-text editing required from user\n\n**Multipart/Alternative:**\n- Every outbound email sent with both text and html parts via nodemailer\n- Nodemailer natively supports this via text and html fields on message object\n\n**Email-Safe Guardrails:**\n- Strip unsupported CSS properties during inlining: position, flex, grid, background-image\n- Ensure inline img tags include explicit width and height HTML attributes (not just CSS sizing)\n- Resolve font-family declarations to web-safe fallback stacks (Arial, Helvetica, sans-serif)\n- Configure juice options to filter unsupported properties\n\n**Sent Message Storage:**\n- Sent copy stored in messages.body_html matches CSS-inlined version transmitted via SMTP\n- Ensures reading pane displays same formatting as recipient sees\n\n**Testing Requirement:**\n- Send real test email containing bold text, bulleted list, color change, inline image, hyperlink\n- Manually verify correct rendering in both Gmail and Outlook (desktop or web)\n\n### 4.15 Rich Text Signature Editor\n\n**Shared Component Architecture:**\n- Signature editor reuses the same Tiptap-based rich text editor component from Compose\n- Toolbar component is shared between Compose and Signatures Management, not duplicated\n- Ensures consistent editing experience and prevents drift between implementations\n\n**Image Handling:**\n- Images inserted via same upload mechanism as Compose (Supabase Storage)\n- After insertion, image displays resize handles (corner-drag) for direct in-editor resizing\n- Maximum width cap enforced (e.g., 300px) to prevent oversized signature images\n- Resized dimensions saved as explicit width and height HTML attributes on <img> tag\n- Prevents accidental inclusion of full-size images in signatures\n\n**Live Preview:**\n- Preview panel displays signature as it will render after CSS-inlining pipeline is applied\n- Preview updates in real-time as user edits\n- Ensures WYSIWYG experience matching final sent output\n- Uses same juice library logic as email-safe send pipeline\n\n**Data Storage:**\n- Signature content stored as body_html in signatures table (HTML format)\n- Inline images stored via Supabase Storage, referenced in body_html\n- Signature auto-appended to outgoing emails as before (existing logic unchanged)\n\n**Scope Boundaries:**\n- Changes only affect signature management screen in Settings\n- No modifications to Compose itself, inbox rendering, calendar, sync logic, AI features, or other areas\n- Existing signature auto-append behavior preserved\n\n### 4.16 Signature Placeholder Bulk Update\n\n**Placeholder Definition:**\n- Placeholders are text tokens in signature body_html (e.g., [Job Title], [Department], [Phone])\n- Used to dynamically populate user-specific information in signatures\n\n**Bulk Update Process:**\n- HR admin selects placeholder from dropdown\n- System displays all users with signatures containing that placeholder\n- HR admin selects target users via checkboxes\n- HR admin enters new value for placeholder\n- System shows before/after preview for selected users\n- On submit, Edge Function bulk-update-placeholders replaces placeholder with new value in signatures.body_html for all selected users\n\n**Update Logic:**\n- Simple string replacement: find placeholder token, replace with new value\n- Preserves all other signature formatting and content\n- Updates only selected users, leaves others unchanged\n\n**Error Handling:**\n- If update fails for any user, log error and continue with remaining users\n- Return success/failure status for each user to display in UI\n\n## 5. Exception and Boundary Cases\n\n| Scenario | Handling |\n|----------|----------|\n| IMAP connection failure | Set sync_status=\'error\', last_error; retry with exponential backoff |\n| UIDVALIDITY reset | Re-sync entire mailbox, update imap_uidvalidity for all messages |\n| Duplicate message (same UID) | Skip insert due to unique constraint |\n| Attachment upload failure | Retry upload; show error toast if persistent failure |\n| SMTP send failure | Show error toast with reason; do not insert sent message |\n| AI API failure (summarization) | Show error message; allow retry |\n| AI API failure (smart search) | Fallback to plain Postgres full-text search |\n| Spam flag user action | Update spam_flags.user_action; if \'confirmed\', move to spam folder |\n| Snooze time passes | Thread reappears at top of inbox |\n| Rule condition match failure | Skip rule, log error |\n| Scheduled send time passes | Send immediately via email-safe pipeline; if send fails, mark status as failed |\n| User deletes mailbox | Cascade delete all associated threads, messages, attachments |\n| Non-admin accesses /admin routes | Redirect to /inbox |\n| Optimistic UI rollback | Show error toast, revert UI state |\n| Notification permission denied | Disable push notifications, show message in settings |\n| Service worker registration failure | Log error, disable push notifications |\n| Push notification send failure | Log error, do not block sync process |\n| Landing page accessed by authenticated user | Redirect to /inbox |\n| /login accessed by authenticated user | Redirect to /inbox |\n| Compose window minimize | Collapse to title bar, preserve draft state |\n| Compose window full-screen toggle | Expand to fill viewport, toggle back to docked position |\n| Rich text formatting in plain-text-only client | Graceful degradation to plain text via multipart/alternative |\n| Inline image insert failure | Show error toast, allow retry |\n| Non-INBOX folder sync failure | Log error, continue syncing other folders |\n| Sent folder polling detects new message | Insert message with folder_id for Sent folder |\n| Drafts folder polling detects changes | Update existing draft or insert new draft |\n| CSS inlining library failure | Log error, send original HTML without inlining; show warning toast |\n| Plain text generation failure | Log error, send HTML-only email; show warning toast |\n| juice library incompatible with Deno | Move SMTP send to Node.js sync service |\n| html-to-text library incompatible with Deno | Move SMTP send to Node.js sync service |\n| Email-safe pipeline strips critical formatting | Log warning, send processed version; user can view sent copy in reading pane |\n| Multiple IMAP connections per mailbox | Maintain separate connection pool per folder; handle connection limits gracefully |\n| SMTP-sent message appears in IMAP Sent folder | Deduplication constraint prevents duplicate insert |\n| Resource booking conflict | Display warning in event form; allow override or cancel |\n| Recurring event edit prompt | Show modal: \\"Edit this occurrence\\" or \\"Edit entire series\\" |\n| Reminder delivery failure | Log error, mark reminder_sent_at to prevent retry loop |\n| .ics generation failure | Log error, send email without .ics attachment; show warning toast |\n| External attendee email send failure | Show error toast; event still created in calendar |\n| Task completion toggle failure | Show error toast, revert UI state |\n| Department filter toggle | Client-side filtering, no server request |\n| Calendar event attachment upload failure | Retry upload; show error toast if persistent failure |\n| Free/busy view data load failure | Show error message, allow retry |\n| Resource availability view data load failure | Show error message, allow retry |\n| Signature image resize exceeds max width | Enforce cap at 300px, prevent further expansion |\n| Signature image upload failure | Show error toast, allow retry |\n| Signature preview rendering failure | Show error message, allow retry |\n| Signature save with oversized image | Validate dimensions before save, show warning if exceeds cap |\n| Signature editor component load failure | Show error message, fallback to plain text editor |\n| CSS inlining in signature preview failure | Show error message, display raw HTML as fallback |\n| Bulk placeholder update with no users selected | Show validation error, prevent submit |\n| Bulk placeholder update fails for some users | Log errors, show partial success message with failed user list |\n| Placeholder not found in selected user signatures | Skip user, log warning, continue with remaining users |\n| Edge Function bulk-update-placeholders timeout | Show error toast, allow retry |\n| Non-admin accesses /admin/signature-placeholders | Redirect to /inbox |\n\n## 6. import { serve } from "https://deno.land/std@0.168.0/http/server.ts";
import { createClient } from "https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2.39.3";

const corsHeaders = {
  "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",
  "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type",
};

serve(async (req) => {
  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") {
    return new Response("ok", { headers: corsHeaders });
  }

  try {
    const supabaseClient = createClient(
      Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL") ?? "",
      Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_ANON_KEY") ?? "",
      {
        global: {
          headers: { Authorization: req.headers.get("Authorization")! },
        },
      }
    );

    const { data: { user }, error: userError } = await supabaseClient.auth.getUser();
    if (userError || !user) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: "Unauthorized" }), {
        headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        status: 401,
      });
    }

    // Verify admin
    const { data: staffData } = await supabaseClient
      .from("staff_users")
      .select("role")
      .eq("id", user.id)
      .single();

    if (!staffData || staffData.role !== "admin") {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: "Forbidden: Admin only" }), {
        headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        status: 403,
      });
    }

    const { placeholder, newValue, signatureIds } = await req.json();

    if (!placeholder || typeof newValue !== "string" || !Array.isArray(signatureIds) || signatureIds.length === 0) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: "Missing required fields" }), {
        headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        status: 400,
      });
    }

    // We use service role to update signatures across mailboxes
    const supabaseAdmin = createClient(
      Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL") ?? "",
      Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY") ?? ""
    );

    // Fetch the signatures
    const { data: signatures, error: fetchError } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from("signatures")
      .select("id, body_html")
      .in("id", signatureIds);

    if (fetchError) {
      throw fetchError;
    }

    const results = [];

    for (const sig of signatures) {
      // Very simple string replace
      const updatedHtml = sig.body_html.replace(new RegExp(placeholder.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&'), 'g'), newValue);
      
      const { error: updateError } = await supabaseAdmin
        .from("signatures")
        .update({ body_html: updatedHtml })
        .eq("id", sig.id);

      if (updateError) {
        results.push({ id: sig.id, success: false, error: updateError.message });
      } else {
        results.push({ id: sig.id, success: true });
      }
    }

    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ success: true, results }), {
      headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      status: 200,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: error.message }), {
      headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      status: 500,
    });
  }
});
EOF

COMMAND:
cat << 'EOF' > /workspace/app-cr8gr9rfgsn5/src/pages/AdminSignaturePlaceholdersPage.tsx
import React, { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase/api";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import {
  Card,
  CardContent,
  CardDescription,
  CardHeader,
  CardTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/card";
import {
  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
} from "@/components/ui/select";
import {
  Table,
  TableBody,
  TableCell,
  TableHead,
  TableHeader,
  TableRow,
} from "@/components/ui/table";
import { Checkbox } from "@/components/ui/checkbox";
import { toast } from "sonner";

interface SignatureResult {
  id: string;
  mailbox_id: string;
  body_html: string;
  mailboxes: {
    email_address: string;
    display_name: string | null;
  };
}

const COMMON_PLACEHOLDERS = ["[Job Title]", "[Phone Number]", "[Department]"];

export default function AdminSignaturePlaceholdersPage() {
  const [selectedPlaceholder, setSelectedPlaceholder] = useState<string>("all");
  const [newValue, setNewValue] = useState("");
  const [signatures, setSignatures] = useState<SignatureResult[]>([]);
  const [selectedSignatureIds, setSelectedSignatureIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [isUpdating, setIsUpdating] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchSignatures();
  }, [selectedPlaceholder]);

  const fetchSignatures = async () => {
    setIsLoading(true);
    try {
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from("signatures")
        .select(`
          id,
          mailbox_id,
          body_html,
          mailboxes (
            email_address,
            display_name
          )
        `);

      if (error) throw error;

      let filtered = (data as unknown as SignatureResult[]) || [];
      if (selectedPlaceholder !== "all") {
        filtered = filtered.filter(s => s.body_html.includes(selectedPlaceholder));
      } else {
        // Find any common placeholder
        filtered = filtered.filter(s => COMMON_PLACEHOLDERS.some(p => s.body_html.includes(p)));
      }

      setSignatures(filtered);
      setSelectedSignatureIds(new Set());
    } catch (error: any) {
      toast.error(`Error fetching signatures: ${error.message}`);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSelectAll = (checked: boolean) => {
    if (checked) {
      setSelectedSignatureIds(new Set(signatures.map(s => s.id)));
    } else {
      setSelectedSignatureIds(new Set());
    }
  };

  const handleSelectSignature = (id: string, checked: boolean) => {
    const next = new Set(selectedSignatureIds);
    if (checked) {
      next.add(id);
    } else {
      next.delete(id);
    }
    setSelectedSignatureIds(next);
  };

  const handleBulkUpdate = async () => {
    if (selectedPlaceholder === "all") {
      toast.error("Please select a specific placeholder first.");
      return;
    }
    if (!newValue.trim()) {
      toast.error("Please enter a new value for the placeholder.");
      return;
    }
    if (selectedSignatureIds.size === 0) {
      toast.error("Please select at least one signature to update.");
      return;
    }

    setIsUpdating(true);
    try {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke("bulk-update-placeholders", {
        body: {
          placeholder: selectedPlaceholder,
          newValue: newValue,
          signatureIds: Array.from(selectedSignatureIds)
        }
      });

      if (error) throw error;
      
      const successCount = data.results.filter((r: any) => r.success).length;
      toast.success(`Successfully updated ${successCount} signatures.`);
      setNewValue("");
      fetchSignatures();
    } catch (error: any) {
      toast.error(`Error updating placeholders: ${error.message}`);
    } finally {
      setIsUpdating(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="container mx-auto py-8 space-y-6">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">Bulk Update Signature Placeholders</h1>
      </div>

      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Filter and Update</CardTitle>
          <CardDescription>Select a placeholder text to find all signatures that contain it, then replace it with a new value for selected users.</CardDescription>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="space-y-4">
          <div className="flex items-end gap-4 max-w-2xl">
            <div className="space-y-2 flex-1">
              <label className="text-sm font-medium">Placeholder</label>
              <Select value={selectedPlaceholder} onValueChange={setSelectedPlaceholder}>
                <SelectTrigger>
                  <SelectValue placeholder="Select placeholder" />
                </SelectTrigger>
                <SelectContent>
                  <SelectItem value="all">Any Placeholder</SelectItem>
                  {COMMON_PLACEHOLDERS.map(p => (
                    <SelectItem key={p} value={p}>{p}</SelectItem>
                  ))}
                </SelectContent>
              </Select>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2 flex-1">
              <label className="text-sm font-medium">New Value</label>
              <Input 
                placeholder="e.g. Senior Engineer" 
                value={newValue}
                onChange={e => setNewValue(e.target.value)}
                disabled={selectedPlaceholder === "all"}
              />
            </div>
            <Button 
              onClick={handleBulkUpdate} 
              disabled={isUpdating || selectedSignatureIds.size === 0 || selectedPlaceholder === "all"}
              className="w-32"
            >
              {isUpdating ? "Updating..." : "Update Selected"}
            </Button>
          </div>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Signatures ({signatures.length})</CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          {isLoading ? (
            <div className="text-center py-8 text-muted-foreground">Loading...</div>
          ) : signatures.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="text-center py-8 text-muted-foreground">No signatures found containing the selected placeholder.</div>
          ) : (
            <div className="overflow-x-auto">
              <Table>
                <TableHeader>
                  <TableRow>
                    <TableHead className="w-[50px]">
                      <Checkbox 
                        checked={selectedSignatureIds.size === signatures.length && signatures.length > 0}
                        onCheckedChange={handleSelectAll}
                      />
                    </TableHead>
                    <TableHead>Email</TableHead>
                    <TableHead>Preview Snippet</TableHead>
                  </TableRow>
                </TableHeader>
                <TableBody>
                  {signatures.map((sig) => {
                    // Extract a snippet of text around the placeholder to show context
                    const htmlContext = sig.body_html.replace(/<[^>]+>/g, ' ');
                    const idx = selectedPlaceholder !== "all" 
                      ? htmlContext.indexOf(selectedPlaceholder)
                      : Math.max(...COMMON_PLACEHOLDERS.map(p => htmlContext.indexOf(p)));
                    
                    const snippetStart = Math.max(0, idx - 30);
                    const snippet = htmlContext.substring(snippetStart, idx + 50);

                    return (
                      <TableRow key={sig.id}>
                        <TableCell>
                          <Checkbox 
                            checked={selectedSignatureIds.has(sig.id)}
                            onCheckedChange={(checked) => handleSelectSignature(sig.id, checked as boolean)}
                          />
                        </TableCell>
                        <TableCell className="font-medium">
                          {sig.mailboxes?.email_address}
                        </TableCell>
                        <TableCell className="text-muted-foreground text-sm max-w-md truncate">
                          ...{snippet}...
                        </TableCell>
                      </TableRow>
                    );
                  })}
                </TableBody>
              </Table>
            </div>
          )}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}
EOF

</action>

## What to do

2. For each HTML file, read its raw contents (they're self-contained — logo is embedded as a base64 data URI, no external assets to fetch).
3. Locate the matching user account in the Frimps Mail schema using the mapping above.
4. Store the HTML content as that user's default signature (whatever field/table the signature settings feature already uses — e.g. `users.signature_html` or a `signatures` table keyed by user id). Set it so the signature auto-populates in the compose window for that user only.
5. Do **not** apply any signature globally or as a fallback default for accounts not in this list.
6. Do **not** touch any existing routes, layout, or unrelated user settings — this is additive only.

     1 import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState, useCallback, type ReactNode } from 'react';
     2 import { supabase } from '@/db/supabase';
     3 import type { User, Session } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
     4 import type { StaffUser, Organization } from '@/types/types';
     5 
     6 export type MfaStatus = 'loading' | 'enrolled' | 'not_enrolled';
     7 
     8 interface AuthContextType {
     9   user: User | null;
    10   session: Session | null;
    11   staffUser: StaffUser | null;
    12   organization: Organization | null;
    13   loading: boolean;
    14   /** Whether the current session has passed the AAL2 (MFA) challenge */
    15   mfaVerified: boolean;
    16   /** Whether the user has an active TOTP factor enrolled */
    17   mfaStatus: MfaStatus;
    18   signIn: (email: string, password: string) => Promise<{ error: string | null }>;
    19   signOut: () => Promise<void>;
    20   refreshStaffUser: () => Promise<void>;
    21   /** Re-check MFA AAL level — call after completing MFA verify/enroll */
    22   refreshMfaStatus: () => Promise<void>;
    23 }
    24 
    25 const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextType | null>(null);
    26 
    27 export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
    28   const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
    29   const [session, setSession] = useState<Session | null>(null);
    30   const [staffUser, setStaffUser] = useState<StaffUser | null>(null);
    31   const [organization, setOrganization] = useState<Organization | null>(null);
    32   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    33   const [mfaVerified, setMfaVerified] = useState(false);
    34   const [mfaStatus, setMfaStatus] = useState<MfaStatus>('loading');
    35 
    36   const checkMfaLevel = useCallback(async () => {
    37     try {
    38       const { data: aalData } = await supabase.auth.mfa.getAuthenticatorAssuranceLevel();
    39       if (!aalData) { setMfaVerified(false); setMfaStatus('loading'); return; }
    40       // AAL2 means MFA challenge was passed this session
    41       const verified = aalData.currentLevel === 'aal2';
    42       setMfaVerified(verified);
    43       // nextLevel === 'aal2' means at least one factor is enrolled
    44       const enrolled = aalData.nextLevel === 'aal2' || aalData.currentLevel === 'aal2';
    45       setMfaStatus(enrolled ? 'enrolled' : 'not_enrolled');
    46     } catch {
    47       setMfaVerified(false);
    48       setMfaStatus('not_enrolled');
    49     }
    50   }, []);
    51 
    52   const fetchStaffUser = useCallback(async (userId: string) => {
    53     const { data, error } = await supabase
    54       .from('staff_users')
    55       .select('*')
    56       .eq('id', userId)
    57       .maybeSingle();
    58     if (!error && data) {
    59       setStaffUser(data as StaffUser);
    60       const { data: org } = await supabase
    61         .from('organizations')
    62         .select('*')
    63         .eq('id', data.organization_id)
    64         .maybeSingle();
    65       if (org) setOrganization(org as Organization);
    66     }
    67   }, []);
    68 
    69   useEffect(() => {
    70     const timeout = setTimeout(() => setLoading(false), 5000);
    71 
    72     supabase.auth.getSession().then(async ({ data: { session } }) => {
    73       clearTimeout(timeout);
    74       setSession(session);
    75       setUser(session?.user ?? null);
    76       if (session?.user) {
    77         await Promise.all([fetchStaffUser(session.user.id), checkMfaLevel()]);
    78         setLoading(false);
    79       } else {
    80         setLoading(false);
    81       }
    82     }).catch(() => {
    83       clearTimeout(timeout);
    84       setLoading(false);
    85     });
    86 
    87     const { data: { subscription } } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange(async (_event, session) => {
    88       setSession(session);
    89       setUser(session?.user ?? null);
    90       if (session?.user) {
    91         fetchStaffUser(session.user.id);
    92         checkMfaLevel();
    93       } else {
    94         setStaffUser(null);
    95         setOrganization(null);
    96         setMfaVerified(false);
    97         setMfaStatus('loading');
    98       }
    99     });
   100 
   101     return () => subscription.unsubscribe();
   102   }, [fetchStaffUser, checkMfaLevel]);
   103 
   104   const signIn = async (email: string, password: string) => {
   105     const { error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({ email, password });
   106     if (error) return { error: error.message };
   107     return { error: null };
   108   };
   109 
   110   const signOut = async () => {
   111     await supabase.auth.signOut();
   112     setStaffUser(null);
   113     setOrganization(null);
   114     setMfaVerified(false);
   115     setMfaStatus('loading');
   116   };
   117 
   118   const refreshStaffUser = async () => {
   119     if (user) await fetchStaffUser(user.id);
   120   };
   121 
   122   const refreshMfaStatus = async () => {
   123     await checkMfaLevel();
   124     if (user) await fetchStaffUser(user.id);
   125   };
   126 
   127   return (
   128     <AuthContext.Provider value={{
   129       user, session, staffUser, organization, loading,
   130       mfaVerified, mfaStatus,
   131       signIn, signOut, refreshStaffUser, refreshMfaStatus,
   132     }}>
   133       {children}
   134     </AuthContext.Provider>
   135   );
   136 }
   137 
   138 export function useAuth() {
   139   const ctx = useContext(AuthContext);
   140   if (!ctx) throw new Error('useAuth must be used within AuthProvider');
   141   return ctx;
   142 }
   143

  useEffect(() => {
    const timeout = setTimeout(() => setLoading(false), 5000);

    supabase.auth.getSession().then(async ({ data: { session } }) => {
      clearTimeout(timeout);
      setSession(session);
      setUser(session?.user ?? null);
      if (session?.user) {
        await Promise.all([fetchStaffUser(session.user.id), checkMfaLevel()]);
        setLoading(false);
      } else {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    }).catch((err) => {
      clearTimeout(timeout);
      setLoading(false);
      // Supabase's gotrue-js may throw AbortError when another tab/request
      // steals the navigator lock. This is a transient race condition, not a
      // real auth failure; leaving state as logged-out is safe.
      if (err?.name !== 'AbortError') {
        console.error('Auth session initialization error:', err);
      }
    });

    const { data: { subscription } } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange(async (_event, session) => {
      setSession(session);
      setUser(session?.user ?? null);
      if (session?.user) {
        fetchStaffUser(session.user.id);
        checkMfaLevel();
      } else {
        setStaffUser(null);
        setOrganization(null);
        setMfaVerified(false);
        setMfaStatus('loading');
      }
    });

    return () => subscription.unsubscribe();
  }, [fetchStaffUser, checkMfaLevel]);

SkillObservation(content='Base directory for this skill: /workspace/app-cr8gr9rfgsn5/.skills/supabase-server\n\n# Supabase Backend\n\nApplies to all frontend stacks (Web / MiniProgram / App). Covers Supabase server-side Schema, RLS, Edge Functions, Storage, Realtime, and Auth configuration.\n\n- For **WeChat Mini-Program/Weapp**, read `references/miniprogram.md` (weapp-specific backend rules: persistence, anon RLS guard, remote-asset transfer, QR code generation).\n\n**Timezone**: Supabase runs in **UTC** by default. All `timestamptz` values are stored in UTC, `now()` returns UTC, and `pg_cron` schedules are interpreted in UTC. Convert to the user\'s local timezone in the frontend or via `AT TIME ZONE` in SQL when display/scheduling requires a specific offset.\n\n## Initial Setup\n\n- Use `supabase_init` to initialize the database or start it from a paused state\n\n## Schema Design\n\n- Minimize tables and columns; merge functionalities into single tables when possible\n- Set database-level default values for required fields\n  - Example: `owner_id uuid not null default auth.uid()`\n  - Example: `order_number text UNIQUE NOT NULL` — use a trigger bound to insert logic to auto-generate the order number\n- For multi-table relations, define them clearly to avoid missing link fields\n- ENUM values require explicit casts\n- **NEVER interact with `supabase/migrations/` directory** (including ls, cat, view)\n- Always apply changes through incremental migrations\n- Verify database state by querying table structure using `supabase_execute_sql`\n- Try to combine tasks and execute all SQL in a single run\n- Always consider performance and speed optimization\n- Disallow SQL statements with silent or ambiguous success semantics (e.g., `IF NOT EXISTS`, `IF EXISTS`) — all operations must fail explicitly when schema or data deviates from expectations\n\n## Data Insertion\n\n**Must-Add:**\n- System-required data (e.g., categories, form dropdown options)\n- **For image URLs**: Use `image_search` to get real URLs — **NEVER** use placeholder URLs like `https://placeholder-image.jpg`\n- Add initial seed data (INSERT INTO) in the migration SQL, using `image_search` for real image URLs\n\n**Allowed:**\n- Homepage display data (e.g., showcases, course previews, products)\n- Demo data (e.g., sample exam sets)\n\n**Forbidden:**\n- User data\n- User activity history (e.g., lottery, orders)\n- Real personal names or any privacy-related info\n\n## SQL Modification\n\n- If you have full context in memory, prioritize using it directly\n- If unsure about current data structure, query it first; do not rely on migration files\n- Under no circumstances should you view or modify migration files\n- If frontend requires a feature without a corresponding table, policy, or edge function, you may add it afterward\n- When modifying business data (non-DDL, policy, RPC, etc.), use `supabase_execute_sql`\n- Before writing api.ts, read the corresponding `supabase/migrations/*.sql` to confirm NOT NULL constraints for every column — context compression may lose field details; migrations are the ground truth\n\n## Security Policies (RLS)\n\n### Design Principles\n\n- **Every table MUST have RLS enabled** — no exceptions, regardless of whether login auth exists\n- **Full coverage per table**: Explicitly define policies for every combination of SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE × {anon, authenticated, admin}. **Never leave any blank** — missing policies silently return empty data or 0 affected rows\n  - Per-table review checklist: anon × {S/I/U/D}, authenticated × {S/I/U/D}, admin × {S/I/U/D}\n  - Anticipate future features: add corresponding constraints now for role×operation combinations that may be needed later\n- **INSERT always requires a matching SELECT**: Any role granted INSERT permission MUST also have a SELECT policy defined\n  - Example: If a policy "allows anyone to submit", add one so "submitters can view their own submissions"\n- Frontend and backend permissions must be aligned\n  - No permission, no interaction\n  - All submit-capable forms must have explicit, role-based permissions\n- PostgREST silently returns 0 affected rows (no error) when UPDATE/DELETE does not match RLS — writes appear to succeed but nothing changed. Tables the frontend needs to mutate must have explicit UPDATE/DELETE policies defined\n- Clear role boundaries\n  - Admins must have full read/write access\n  - Roles must have complete and secure permissions defined for every possible interaction scenario\n- All internal checks must use `SECURITY DEFINER`\n\n### Implementation Notes\n\n- RLS should only depend on **static columns or secure functions**\n- **Policy self-loop**: A policy\'s USING clause must not query the same table it protects — use a `SECURITY DEFINER` helper function instead (it bypasses RLS when querying the table)\n- Use public views to expose read-only access to selected fields of high-privilege tables\n- **The column checked by `auth.uid()` MUST auto-fill**: For each INSERT policy whose WITH CHECK contains `<col> = auth.uid()`, that exact `<col>` MUST be declared `NOT NULL DEFAULT auth.uid()`. The client never sends it. Apply this to that one column only — never blanket-default other uuid/FK columns (e.g. `assigned_to`), which would be a bug.\n  - ❌ `owner_id uuid NOT NULL` + client `.insert(payload)` → owner is NULL → "new row violates row-level security policy"\n  - ✅ `owner_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT auth.uid()`\n\n## Storage Buckets\n\n- Create for image/file uploads only\n- **CRITICAL**: NEVER store images/videos as Base64 in database\n- ALL image/video assets MUST be uploaded to Supabase Storage\n- When third-party APIs return image/video URLs, transfer to Storage in Edge Function\n- buckets must be created in migration SQL via `supabase_apply_migration` — **do not** create them dynamically inside Edge Functions\n- Frontend validation: 1MB limit, snake_case filenames\n\n\n## Edge Functions\n\n### When to Use\n\n- **MANDATORY for third-party API calls**: ALL external API integrations (payment, weather, maps, etc.) MUST be implemented through Edge Functions\n- **STRICTLY FORBIDDEN**: Direct third-party API calls from client-side code (fetch/axios in frontend components)\n- Server-side secrets and service-role operations\n- Sensitive payment logic requiring secure environment\n\n### Authoring\n\n- Create files under `supabase/functions/<function_name>/`\n- **CRITICAL**: Use `supabase_deploy_edge_function` to deploy after modifying or creating Edge Function files\n- Put cross-domain content in the `_shared/` directory\n- No URL imports — only npm and JSR\n- Do not add secrets unless explicitly required by API documentation\n- Handle CORS properly\n- `supabase.sql` forbidden in Edge Functions\n- `supabase.raw` forbidden in Edge Functions — use `.rpc()` for atomic operations\n- Response Content-Type must be chosen only from: `[\'application/json\', \'application/octet-stream\', \'text/event-stream\', \'multipart/form-data\']`\n- Make proper use of optimistic locking to ensure that subsequent settlement operations in the state flow are executed only once, even if called multiple times\n\n\n## Supabase Auth\n\n- Your knowledge may be outdated. **MUST** call `skill_action(skill="login")` to refresh with the latest information\n- Any form of third-party login (OAuth or SSO) must use the login skill as the knowledge basis\n\n## Scheduled Tasks\n\nWhenever the app needs work to run **on a time schedule** or **automatically after a condition is met** (with no user action driving it), use a scheduled task. Typical cases:\n\n- **Scheduled notifications** — daily reminders, expiry/renewal alerts, fixed-time push\n- **Scheduled refresh** — refresh cached aggregates, sync external data, recompute rankings periodically\n- **Scheduled data analysis** — nightly report generation, periodic statistics roll-ups\n- **Conditional triggers** — "after condition X, do Y", e.g. *when traffic > 100, send a notification*; *when stock < threshold, alert*; *when an order is unpaid for 30 min, cancel it*\n\n**All of the above MUST use the official `pg_cron` pattern** — a `pg_cron` job periodically runs a database function that scans for due/matching rows and updates their state; for third-party side effects (push, email, external APIs) the job invokes an Edge Function via `pg_net`. The database only queries and updates state; notification/third-party logic stays in the Edge Function. Do **NOT** drive such work from frontend timers or rely on a user opening a page.\n\n**Timezone**: `pg_cron` schedules run in **UTC**. Compute the UTC cron expression from the intended local time (e.g. 9:00 in UTC+8 → `0 1 * * *`).\n\nWhen building any scheduled task, read `references/scheduled-tasks.md`.\n\n## Supabase Realtime (low-frequency feature)\n\nWhen realtime capability is genuinely needed, read `references/realtime.md` — it covers server-side publication switches, per-stack client subscription patterns, and RN/weapp limitations.\n\n**Multi-identity scenarios**: Identity selection / role assignment logic **must NOT** be placed on the frontend — it **MUST** be encapsulated in an Edge Function.', observation=<ObservationType.SKILL: 'skill'>, skill='supabase-server', success=True, data={'object_storage_path': 'skill-aws-online:aws-prod/miaoda_skills/prompts/system/supabase-server/'})

const key = 'eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiIsInR5cCI6IkpXVCJ9.eyJpc3MiOiJzdXBhYmFzZSIsInJlZiI6Imhnenl5cHlxYXdjcHBpdm5naHByIiwicm9sZSI6InNlcnZpY2Vfcm9sZSIsImlhdCI6MTc4MzA0NDE3MCwiZXhwIjoyMDk4NjIwMTcwfQ.U8lrNfsqklbAzYa0jmjo3wWPpWmFazWDeRY47kGhYBs';

  const { data: contacts, error: cErr } = await supabase.from('contacts').select('id, email, name, created_at');

}

main().catch(console.error);

const { createClient } = require('@supabase/supabase-js');

const url = 'https://hgzyypyqawcppivnghpr.supabase.co';
const key = 'eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiIsInR5cCI6IkpXVCJ9.eyJpc3MiOiJzdXBhYmFzZSIsInJlZiI6Imhnenl5cHlxYXdjcHBpdm5naHByIiwicm9sZSI6InNlcnZpY2Vfcm9sZSIsImlhdCI6MTc4MzA0NDE3MCwiZXhwIjoyMDk4NjIwMTcwfQ.U8lrNfsqklbAzYa0jmjo3wWPpWmFazWDeRY47kGhYBs';

async function main() {
  console.log('=== staff_users ===');
  const { data: staff, error: staffErr } = await supabase.from('staff_users').select('id, full_name, role, organization_id, created_at, is_active');
  if (staffErr) console.error(staffErr); else console.table(staff);

  console.log('=== mailboxes ===');
  const { data: mailboxes, error: mbErr } = await supabase.from('mailboxes').select('id, local_part, display_name, status, created_at');
  if (mbErr) console.error(mbErr); else console.table(mailboxes);

  console.log('=== auth.users ===');
  const { data: authUsers, error: authErr } = await supabase.auth.admin.listUsers();
  if (authErr) console.error(authErr); else console.table(authUsers.users.map(u => ({ id: u.id, email: u.email, created_at: u.created_at })));
}

main().catch(console.error);

cat > /workspace/app-cr8gr9rfgsn5/tasks/delete-demo-data.cjs << 'EOF'
const { createClient } = require('@supabase/supabase-js');

const url = 'https://hgzyypyqawcppivnghpr.supabase.co';
const key = 'eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiIsInR5cCI6IkpXVCJ9.eyJpc3MiOiJzdXBhYmFzZSIsInJlZiI6Imhnenl5cHlxYXdjcHBpdm5naHByIiwicm9sZSI6InNlcnZpY2Vfcm9sZSIsImlhdCI6MTc4MzA0NDE3MCwiZXhwIjoyMDk4NjIwMTcwfQ.U8lrNfsqklbAzYa0jmjo3wWPpWmFazWDeRY47kGhYBs';

const supabase = createClient(url, key);

const deleted = {
  contacts: [],
  mailbox: null,
  staffUser: null,
  authUser: null,
  signatures: [],
};

async function main() {
  // 1. Delete demo contacts (created by seed, external companies)
  const { data: contacts, error: contactsErr } = await supabase.from('contacts').select('id, email, name').in('email', [
    'logistics@totalenergies.com',
    'contracts@goil.com.gh',
    'invoicing@shell.com.gh',
    'hr@frimpsoil.com.gh',
    'corporate@gcbbank.com.gh',
    'info@kwesifillingstation.com',
    'hse@energycom.gov.gh',
    'e.tetteh@frimpsoil.com.gh'
  ]);
  if (contactsErr) throw contactsErr;

  if (contacts && contacts.length > 0) {
    const ids = contacts.map(c => c.id);
    const { error } = await supabase.from('contacts').delete().in('id', ids);
    if (error) throw error;
    deleted.contacts = contacts;
  }

  // 2. Delete demo mailbox (Kofi Mensah seed)
  const { data: mailbox } = await supabase.from('mailboxes').select('id, email_address, display_name').eq('id', 'b0000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001').maybeSingle();
  if (mailbox) {
    // Also delete related signatures for this mailbox
    const { data: sigs } = await supabase.from('signatures').select('id, mailbox_id').eq('mailbox_id', mailbox.id);
    if (sigs && sigs.length > 0) {
      const { error: sigErr } = await supabase.from('signatures').delete().eq('mailbox_id', mailbox.id);
      if (sigErr) throw sigErr;
      deleted.signatures = sigs;
    }
    const { error: mbErr } = await supabase.from('mailboxes').delete().eq('id', mailbox.id);
    if (mbErr) throw mbErr;
    deleted.mailbox = mailbox;
  }

  // 3. Delete demo staff user
  const { data: staffUser } = await supabase.from('staff_users').select('id, full_name, role, organization_id').eq('id', 'd0000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001').maybeSingle();
  if (staffUser) {
    const { error: staffErr } = await supabase.from('staff_users').delete().eq('id', staffUser.id);
    if (staffErr) throw staffErr;
    deleted.staffUser = staffUser;
  }

  // 4. Delete demo auth user
  const { data: authUser, error: authErr } = await supabase.auth.admin.getUserById('d0000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001');
  if (authErr) throw authErr;
  if (authUser.user) {
    const { error: delAuthErr } = await supabase.auth.admin.deleteUser(authUser.user.id);
    if (delAuthErr) throw delAuthErr;
    deleted.authUser = { id: authUser.user.id, email: authUser.user.email };
  }

  console.log('DELETED RECORDS SUMMARY');
  console.log(JSON.stringify(deleted, null, 2));
}

main().catch(err => {
  console.error('ERROR:', err);
  process.exit(1);
});
EOF

     9 serve(async (req) => {
    10   if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') return new Response('ok', { headers: corsHeaders });
    11 
    12   try {
    13     const supabase = createClient(
    14       Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL')!,
    15       Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY')!,
    16     );
    17 
    18     const { mailbox_id, to, cc, bcc, subject, body_html, reply_to_message_id, attachments } = await req.json();
    19 
    20     if (!mailbox_id || !to?.length || !subject) {
    21       return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'mailbox_id, to[], subject required' }), {
    22         status: 400, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    23       });
    24     }
    25 
    26     // Verify mailbox exists
    27     const { data: mailbox, error: mbError } = await supabase
    28       .from('mailboxes')
    29       .select('id')
    30       .eq('id', mailbox_id)
    31       .single();
    32     if (mbError || !mailbox) throw new Error('Mailbox not found');
    33 
    34     // Queue outbound message for the sync service email-safe pipeline
    35     const { error: insertErr } = await supabase.from('outbound_messages').insert({
    36       mailbox_id,
    37       to_addresses: to as string[],
    38       cc_addresses: (cc ?? []) as string[],
    39       bcc_addresses: (bcc ?? []) as string[],
    40       subject,
    41       body_html,
    42       reply_to_message_id: reply_to_message_id ?? null,
    43       attachments_json: attachments ?? [],
    44       status: 'pending',
    45     });
    46 
    47     if (insertErr) throw new Error(`Failed to queue outbound message: ${insertErr.message}`);
    48 
    49     return new Response(JSON.stringify({ success: true, queued: true }), {
    50       headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    51     });
    52   } catch (err) {
    53     return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: String(err) }), {

    18 const SUPABASE_URL = process.env.SUPABASE_URL!;
    19 const SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY = process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!;
    20 const BACKFILL_DAYS = parseInt(process.env.BACKFILL_DAYS ?? '90', 10);
    21 const POLL_INTERVAL_MS = parseInt(process.env.POLL_INTERVAL_MS ?? '30000', 10);
    22 const SCHEDULED_CHECK_INTERVAL = 60_000;   // 1 minute
    23 const PENDING_POLL_INTERVAL    = 120_000;  // 2 minutes — re-check for newly credentialed mailboxes
    24 const OUTBOUND_POLL_INTERVAL   = 10_000;   // 10 seconds — quick outbound send queue

    33 async function processMessage(
    34   supabase: SupabaseClient,
    35   mailboxId: string,
    36   folderIdMap: Map<string, string>,
    37   msg: ParsedMessage,
    38 ): Promise<void> {
    39   const folderId = folderIdMap.get(msg.inboxFolderName) ?? null;
    40 
    41   // Extract message-id references for threading
    42   const referencedIds = [
    43     msg.headers['in-reply-to'],
    44     ...(msg.headers['references']?.split(/\s+/) ?? []),
    45   ].filter(Boolean);
    46 
    47   const threadId = await findOrCreateThread(
    48     supabase,
    49     mailboxId,
    50     msg.subject,
    51     msg.fromAddress,
    52     msg.toAddresses,
    53     referencedIds,
    54     folderId,
    55     msg.sentAt,
    56   );
    57 
    58   const messageId = await insertMessage(supabase, mailboxId, threadId, msg, folderId);
    59   if (!messageId) return; // Duplicate, skip
    60 
    61   // Store attachments
    62   if (msg.attachments.length) {
    63     await storeAttachments(supabase, mailboxId, messageId, msg.attachments);
    64   }
    65 
    66   // Spam detection (inbox messages only)
    67   if (!msg.isSpamFolder) {
    68     const headerAnalysis = analyzeSpamHeaders(msg.headers);
    69     if (headerAnalysis && headerAnalysis.is_spam) {
    70       // Layer 1: confirmed spam from headers
    71       await supabase.from('spam_flags').insert({
    72         message_id: messageId,
    73         source: 'spamassassin',
    74         confidence: headerAnalysis.confidence,
    75         reason: headerAnalysis.reason,
    76         user_action: 'pending',
    77       });
    78     } else if (needsAISecondPass(msg.headers)) {
    79       // Layer 2: AI second-pass for borderline / header-less
    80       const aiAnalysis = await analyzeSpamWithAI(msg.subject, msg.fromAddress, msg.bodyText);
    81       if (aiAnalysis.confidence >= 0.6) {
    82         await supabase.from('spam_flags').insert({
    83           message_id: messageId,
    84           source: 'ai_second_pass',
    85           confidence: aiAnalysis.confidence,
    86           reason: aiAnalysis.reason,
    87           user_action: 'pending',
    88         });
    89       }
    90     }
    91   }
    92 
    93   // Apply matching rules
    94   await applyRules(supabase, mailboxId, messageId, msg);
    95 
    96   // Update thread's last_message_at
    97   await supabase
    98     .from('threads')
    99     .update({ last_message_at: msg.sentAt.toISOString() })
   100     .eq('id', threadId)
   101     .lt('last_message_at', msg.sentAt.toISOString());
   102 }

   104 async function syncMailbox(mailboxRow: {
   105   id: string;
   106   email_address: string;
   107   imap_host: string;
   108   imap_port: number;
   109   credential_vault_ref: string;
   110 }): Promise<void> {
   111   const { id: mailboxId, email_address, imap_host, imap_port, credential_vault_ref } = mailboxRow;
   112   console.log(`[SYNC] Starting sync for ${email_address}`);
   113 
   114   let password: string;
   115   try {
   116     password = await getCredential(supabase, credential_vault_ref);
   117   } catch (err) {
   118     await supabase.from('mailboxes').update({
   119       sync_status: 'error',
   120       last_error: `Vault error: ${err}`,
   121     }).eq('id', mailboxId);
   122     return;
   123   }
   124 
   125   const client = new ImapClient({
   126     id: mailboxId,
   127     emailAddress: email_address,
   128     imapHost: imap_host,
   129     imapPort: imap_port,
   130     password,
   131   }, supabase);
   132 
   133   // High-priority folders get dedicated IMAP connections for near-real-time coverage
   134   const HIGH_PRIORITY_PATTERNS = ['inbox', 'sent', 'drafts', 'archive'];
   135   // Low-priority folders share a single polling connection at a slower interval
   136   const LOW_PRIORITY_PATTERNS = ['junk', 'trash', 'spam'];
   137   const LOW_PRIORITY_POLL_MS = 90_000; // 90 seconds
   138 
   139   // Track all per-folder clients so we can disconnect on error
   140   const folderClients: ImapClient[] = [];
   141 
   142   try {
   143     await client.connect();
   144     await supabase.from('mailboxes').update({ sync_status: 'syncing' }).eq('id', mailboxId);
   145 
   146     // Discover folders using the main client
   147     const folderNames = await client.listFolders();
   148     const folderIdMap = await syncFolders(supabase, mailboxId, folderNames);
   149 
   150     // Backfill all folders sequentially on the main connection
   151     for (const folderName of folderNames) {
   152       console.log(`[SYNC] Backfilling ${email_address}/${folderName}`);
   153       try {
   154         await client.backfill(folderName, BACKFILL_DAYS, async (msg) => {
   155           await processMessage(supabase, mailboxId, folderIdMap, msg);
   156         });
   157       } catch (err) {
   158         console.warn(`[SYNC] Backfill failed for ${email_address}/${folderName}:`, err);
   159       }
   160     }
   161 
   162     await supabase.from('mailboxes').update({
   163       sync_status: 'active',
   164       last_synced_at: new Date().toISOString(),
   165       last_error: null,
   166     }).eq('id', mailboxId);
   167 
   168     console.log(`[SYNC] Backfill complete for ${email_address}, entering multi-connection watch`);
   169 
   170     // Disconnect main client — dedicated per-folder clients take over
   171     await client.disconnect();
   172 
   173     // Categorise available folders
   174     const highPriorityFolders = folderNames.filter(name =>
   175       HIGH_PRIORITY_PATTERNS.some(p => name.toLowerCase().includes(p))
   176     );
   177     const lowPriorityFolders = folderNames.filter(name =>
   178       LOW_PRIORITY_PATTERNS.some(p => name.toLowerCase().includes(p))
   179     );
   180 
   181     const config: MailboxConfig = { id: mailboxId, emailAddress: email_address, imapHost: imap_host, imapPort: imap_port, password };
   182 
   183     // Spawn a dedicated IMAP connection per high-priority folder
   184     const highPriorityPromises = highPriorityFolders.map(async (folderName) => {
   185       const fc = new ImapClient(config, supabase);
   186       folderClients.push(fc);
   187       try {
   188         await fc.connect();
   189         console.log(`[SYNC] Dedicated watcher started for ${email_address}/${folderName}`);
   190         await fc.watchFolders([folderName], async (msg) => {
   191           await processMessage(supabase, mailboxId, folderIdMap, msg);
   192         }, POLL_INTERVAL_MS);
   193       } catch (err) {
   194         console.error(`[SYNC] Watcher error for ${email_address}/${folderName}:`, err);
   195         throw err; // propagate so the outer Promise.race can detect failure
   196       } finally {
   197         await fc.disconnect().catch(() => {});
   198       }
   199     });
   200 
   201     // Spawn one shared polling connection for low-priority folders
   202     let lowPriorityPromise: Promise<void> = Promise.resolve();
   203     if (lowPriorityFolders.length) {
   204       const lpc = new ImapClient(config, supabase);
   205       folderClients.push(lpc);
   206       lowPriorityPromise = (async () => {
   207         await lpc.connect();
   208         console.log(`[SYNC] Low-priority poller started for ${email_address}: ${lowPriorityFolders.join(', ')}`);
   209         await lpc.watchFolders(lowPriorityFolders, async (msg) => {
   210           await processMessage(supabase, mailboxId, folderIdMap, msg);
   211         }, LOW_PRIORITY_POLL_MS);
   212       })().catch(err => {
   213         console.error(`[SYNC] Low-priority poller error for ${email_address}:`, err);
   214         throw err;
   215       }).finally(async () => {
   216         await lpc.disconnect().catch(() => {});
   217       });
   218     }
   219 
   220     // If any folder connection drops, treat the whole mailbox sync as failed (triggers reconnect)
   221     await Promise.race([...highPriorityPromises, lowPriorityPromise]);
   222     throw new Error('A folder watcher exited unexpectedly');
   223 
   224   } catch (err) {
   225     console.error(`[SYNC] Error for ${email_address}:`, err);
   226     await supabase.from('mailboxes').update({
   227       sync_status: 'error',
   228       last_error: String(err),
   229     }).eq('id', mailboxId);
   230     // Clean up all folder clients
   231     await Promise.allSettled(folderClients.map(fc => fc.disconnect()));
   232     await client.disconnect().catch(() => {});
   233   }
   234 }
