   236 /** Build an SmtpClient for a mailbox, fetching the password from Vault */
   237 interface SmtpMailbox {
   238   id: string;
   239   email_address: string;
   240   smtp_host: string;
   241   smtp_port: number;
   242   credential_vault_ref: string;
   243   display_name?: string | null;
   244 }
   245 
   246 async function getSmtpClient(mailbox: SmtpMailbox): Promise<SmtpClient> {
   247   const password = await getCredential(supabase, mailbox.credential_vault_ref);
   248   return new SmtpClient({
   249     host: mailbox.smtp_host,
   250     port: mailbox.smtp_port,
   251     emailAddress: mailbox.email_address,
   252     password,
   253     displayName: mailbox.display_name ?? undefined,
   254   });
   255 }
   256 
   257 /** Resolve reply-to headers for threading from an original message */
   258 async function resolveReplyHeaders(replyToMessageId?: string | null): Promise<{ inReplyTo?: string; references?: string }> {
   259   if (!replyToMessageId) return {};
   260   const { data: origMsg } = await supabase
   261     .from('messages')
   262     .select('raw_headers')
   263     .eq('id', replyToMessageId)
   264     .maybeSingle();
   265   if (!origMsg?.raw_headers) return {};
   266   const headers = origMsg.raw_headers as Record<string, string>;
   267   const inReplyTo = headers['message-id'] ?? headers['Message-ID'];
   268   const references = [headers['references'], inReplyTo].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
   269   return { inReplyTo, references };
   270 }
   271 
   272 /** Insert a sent copy into messages table */
   273 async function recordSentMessage(opts: {
   274   mailbox_id: string;
   275   reply_to_message_id?: string | null;
   276   from_address: string;
   277   from_name?: string | null;
   278   to_addresses: string[];
   279   cc_addresses?: string[];
   280   bcc_addresses?: string[];
   281   subject: string;
   282   body_html: string;
   283   body_text: string;
   284   message_id: string;
   285 }): Promise<void> {
   286   let threadId: string | null = null;
   287 
   288   if (opts.reply_to_message_id) {
   289     const { data: existingThread } = await supabase
   290       .from('messages')
   291       .select('thread_id')
   292       .eq('id', opts.reply_to_message_id)
   293       .maybeSingle();
   294     threadId = existingThread?.thread_id ?? null;
   295   }
   296 
   297   if (!threadId) {
   298     // Create a thread for this outbound message
   299     const participants = [opts.from_address, ...opts.to_addresses, ...(opts.cc_addresses ?? []), ...(opts.bcc_addresses ?? [])];
   300     const { data: thread, error: threadErr } = await supabase
   301       .from('threads')
   302       .insert({
   303         mailbox_id: opts.mailbox_id,
   304         subject: opts.subject,
   305         participants: Array.from(new Set(participants)),
   306         folder_id: null,
   307         last_message_at: new Date().toISOString(),
   308         is_read: true,
   309         is_starred: false,
   310         labels: [],
   311       })
   312       .select('id')
   313       .single();
   314 
   315     if (threadErr) throw new Error(`Failed to create sent thread: ${threadErr.message}`);
   316     threadId = thread?.id ?? null;
   317   }
   318 
   319   const { error } = await supabase.from('messages').insert({
   320     thread_id: threadId,
   321     mailbox_id: opts.mailbox_id,
   322     from_address: opts.from_address,
   323     from_name: opts.from_name ?? null,
   324     to_addresses: opts.to_addresses,
   325     cc_addresses: opts.cc_addresses ?? [],
   326     bcc_addresses: opts.bcc_addresses ?? [],
   327     subject: opts.subject,
   328     body_html: opts.body_html,
   329     body_text: opts.body_text,
   330     sent_at: new Date().toISOString(),
   331     is_read: true,
   332     is_flagged: false,
   333     spam_status: 'clean',
   334     raw_headers: { 'message-id': opts.message_id },
   335   });
   336 
   337   if (error) {
   338     console.error('[SMTP] Failed to record sent message:', error);
   339     throw new Error(`Failed to record sent message: ${error.message}`);
   340   }
   341 }

   343 /** Send an outbound email with the email-safe pipeline and persist a sent copy */
   344 async function sendOutboundEmail(outbound: {
   345   id: string;
   346   mailbox_id: string;
   347   to_addresses: string[];
   348   cc_addresses: string[];
   349   bcc_addresses: string[];
   350   subject: string;
   351   body_html: string;
   352   reply_to_message_id?: string | null;
   353   attachments_json?: unknown;
   354 }, mailbox: {
   355   id: string;
   356   email_address: string;
   357   smtp_host: string;
   358   smtp_port: number;
   359   credential_vault_ref: string;
   360   display_name?: string | null;
   361 }, source: 'outbound' | 'scheduled'): Promise<void> {
   362   const smtp = await getSmtpClient(mailbox);
   363   const { inReplyTo, references } = await resolveReplyHeaders(outbound.reply_to_message_id);
   364 
   365   const attachments: Array<{ filename: string; content: Buffer; mimeType: string }> = [];
   366   const rawAttachments = (outbound.attachments_json ?? []) as Array<{ path: string; filename: string; mimeType?: string }>;
   367   for (const att of rawAttachments) {
   368     if (att?.path) {
   369       const { data: fileData, error: dlErr } = await supabase.storage.from('attachments').download(att.path);
   370       if (fileData && !dlErr) {
   371         const buffer = Buffer.from(await fileData.arrayBuffer());
   372         attachments.push({ filename: att.filename, content: buffer, mimeType: att.mimeType ?? 'application/octet-stream' });
   373       }
   374     }
   375   }
   376 
   377   const result = await smtp.sendMail({
   378     to: outbound.to_addresses,
   379     cc: outbound.cc_addresses,
   380     bcc: outbound.bcc_addresses,
   381     subject: outbound.subject,
   382     htmlBody: outbound.body_html,
   383     inReplyTo,
   384     references,
   385     attachments,
   386   });
   387 
   388   await recordSentMessage({
   389     mailbox_id: outbound.mailbox_id,
   390     reply_to_message_id: outbound.reply_to_message_id,
   391     from_address: mailbox.email_address,
   392     from_name: mailbox.display_name,
   393     to_addresses: outbound.to_addresses,
   394     cc_addresses: outbound.cc_addresses,
   395     bcc_addresses: outbound.bcc_addresses,
   396     subject: outbound.subject,
   397     body_html: result.html,
   398     body_text: result.text,
   399     message_id: result.messageId,
   400   });
   401 
   402   console.log(`[SMTP] ${source} message ${outbound.id} sent as ${result.messageId}`);
   403 }
   404 
   405 /** Process scheduled messages due to be sent */
   406 async function processScheduledMessages(): Promise<void> {
   407   const { data: due } = await supabase
   408     .from('scheduled_messages')
   409     .select('*, mailboxes(email_address, smtp_host, smtp_port, credential_vault_ref, display_name)')
   410     .eq('status', 'pending')
   411     .lte('send_at', new Date().toISOString())
   412     .limit(10);
   413 
   414   if (!due?.length) return;
   415 
   416   for (const scheduled of due) {
   417     try {
   418       const mailbox = (scheduled as Record<string, unknown>).mailboxes as {
   419         email_address: string;
   420         smtp_host: string;
   421         smtp_port: number;
   422         credential_vault_ref: string;
   423         display_name: string;
   424       };
   425 
   426       await sendOutboundEmail(
   427         {
   428           id: scheduled.id as string,
   429           mailbox_id: scheduled.mailbox_id as string,
   430           to_addresses: (scheduled.to_addresses ?? []) as string[],
   431           cc_addresses: (scheduled.cc_addresses ?? []) as string[],
   432           bcc_addresses: (scheduled.bcc_addresses ?? []) as string[],
   433           subject: scheduled.subject as string,
   434           body_html: scheduled.body_html as string,
   435           reply_to_message_id: (scheduled.reply_to_message_id ?? null) as string | null,
   436           attachments_json: scheduled.attachments_json ?? [],
   437         },
   438         { ...mailbox, id: scheduled.mailbox_id as string },
   439         'scheduled',
   440       );
   441 
   442       await supabase.from('scheduled_messages')
   443         .update({ status: 'sent', sent_at: new Date().toISOString() })
   444         .eq('id', scheduled.id);
   445       console.log(`[SCHEDULED] Sent scheduled message ${scheduled.id}`);
   446     } catch (err) {
   447       console.error(`[SCHEDULED] Failed to send ${scheduled.id}:`, err);
   448       await supabase.from('scheduled_messages')
   449         .update({ status: 'failed' })
   450         .eq('id', scheduled.id);
   451     }
   452   }
   453 }
   454 
   455 /** Process queued outbound messages */
   456 async function processOutboundMessages(): Promise<void> {
   457   const { data: pending, error } = await supabase
   458     .from('outbound_messages')
   459     .select('*, mailboxes(email_address, smtp_host, smtp_port, credential_vault_ref, display_name)')
   460     .eq('status', 'pending')
   461     .order('created_at', { ascending: true })
   462     .limit(10);
   463 
   464   if (error) {
   465     console.error('[OUTBOUND] Failed to fetch queue:', error);
   466     return;
   467   }
   468   if (!pending?.length) return;
   469 
   470   for (const msg of pending) {
   471     const outbound = msg as Record<string, unknown>;
   472     const id = outbound.id as string;
   473 
   474     // Mark as sending to avoid duplicate picks from concurrent workers
   475     const { error: lockErr } = await supabase
   476       .from('outbound_messages')
   477       .update({ status: 'sending', updated_at: new Date().toISOString() })
   478       .eq('id', id)
   479       .eq('status', 'pending');
   480     if (lockErr) continue;
   481 
   482     const mailbox = outbound.mailboxes as {
   483       email_address: string;
   484       smtp_host: string;
   485       smtp_port: number;
   486       credential_vault_ref: string;
   487       display_name: string;
   488     };
   489 
   490     try {
   491       await sendOutboundEmail(
   492         {
   493           id,
   494           mailbox_id: outbound.mailbox_id as string,
   495           to_addresses: (outbound.to_addresses ?? []) as string[],
   496           cc_addresses: (outbound.cc_addresses ?? []) as string[],
   497           bcc_addresses: (outbound.bcc_addresses ?? []) as string[],
   498           subject: outbound.subject as string,
   499           body_html: outbound.body_html as string,
   500           reply_to_message_id: (outbound.reply_to_message_id ?? null) as string | null,
   501           attachments_json: outbound.attachments_json ?? [],
   502         },
   503         { ...mailbox, id: outbound.mailbox_id as string },
   504         'outbound',
   505       );
   506 
   507       await supabase.from('outbound_messages')
   508         .update({ status: 'sent', sent_at: new Date().toISOString(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString() })
   509         .eq('id', id);
   510     } catch (err) {
   511       console.error(`[OUTBOUND] Failed to send ${id}:`, err);
   512       await supabase.from('outbound_messages')
   513         .update({ status: 'failed', error: String(err), updated_at: new Date().toISOString() })
   514         .eq('id', id);
   515     }
   516   }
   517 }
   518 
   519 /** Track which mailbox IDs are already being synced to avoid duplicate connections */
   520 const activeSyncIds = new Set<string>();
   521 
   522 async function runMailboxLoop(mb: {
   523   id: string; email_address: string; imap_host: string;
   524   imap_port: number; credential_vault_ref: string;
   525 }, retryDelayMs = 30000): Promise<void> {
   526   try {
   527     await syncMailbox(mb);
   528     // Normal completion (shouldn't happen while watchFolder is alive)
   529     console.log(`[SYNC] Sync ended normally for ${mb.email_address}`);
   530   } catch (err) {
   531     console.error(`[SYNC] Fatal error for ${mb.email_address}:`, err);
   532   } finally {
   533     activeSyncIds.delete(mb.id);
   534   }
   535 
   536   // Auto-reconnect with backoff
   537   console.log(`[SYNC] Reconnecting ${mb.email_address} in ${retryDelayMs / 1000}s`);
   538   await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, retryDelayMs));
   539   await startMailboxSync(mb, Math.min(retryDelayMs * 2, 300000)); // cap at 5 min
   540 }
   541 
   542 function startMailboxSync(mb: {
   543   id: string; email_address: string; imap_host: string;
   544   imap_port: number; credential_vault_ref: string;
   545 }, retryDelayMs = 30000): void {
   546   if (activeSyncIds.has(mb.id)) {
   547     console.log(`[SYNC] Already syncing ${mb.email_address}, skipping`);
   548     return;
   549   }
   550   activeSyncIds.add(mb.id);
   551   // Run the sync loop in the background so the caller isn't blocked
   552   runMailboxLoop(mb, retryDelayMs).catch(err => {
   553     console.error(`[SYNC] Unhandled loop error for ${mb.email_address}:`, err);
   554     activeSyncIds.delete(mb.id);
   555   });
   556 }
   557

    24 export class SmtpClient {

    28   constructor(config: SmtpConfig) {

    52   async sendMail(opts: SendMailOptions): Promise<{ messageId: string; html: string; text: string }> {
    53     const safe = emailSafePipeline(opts.htmlBody);
    54     const info = await this.transporter.sendMail({
    55       from: `"${this.config.displayName ?? ''}" <${this.config.emailAddress}>`,

    67         contentType: a.mimeType,
